先週・先々週（9/14～9/28）発表の主な経済指標
半導体や投資関連財の生産は堅調。AI投資の強さが実体面
にも波及していることを確認。企業部門はなお資金余剰を維持。
外部調達も株式中心で、債務調達の増勢は限定的
割高な米国産原油への代替調達で輸入額が増加する中、原
油タンカー運賃も急騰。中東産への調達回帰が進んでも輸入
負担は下がらず、貿易赤字を通じて円安圧力が残るリスク
注目点② 原油輸送費が急騰。貿易赤字長期化で円安圧力も注目点①旺盛なAI投資が生産を押し上げ。資金調達にも余力

米国：生産は底堅く推移。AI・防衛が投資財生産をけん引、半導体増産も寄与
 2026年8月の鉱工業生産は前年比＋1.4％と底堅い結果
― 半導体関連の生産が引き続きけん引。生産能力が2022年対比1.7倍に拡大するなど生産体制の増強が背景。事実、主
要半導体メーカーによる工場新設・増強を受け、MOSロジック（GPU、CPUなど演算用）の量産が本格化
 AI・防衛分野を中心に投資財生産も堅調さを維持
― 投資関連財はAI・防衛が押し上げる形で同＋4.7％と増勢継続。国防・宇宙関連設備は同＋6.1％ と全体を上回る伸び。
地政学リスクの高まりを背景に軍用機やミサイル・防空システムなどの生産体制強化が図られている模様
4

（兆ドル）
資金余剰
米国：AI投資ブーム下で企業の外部資金調達は拡大も、債務調達の増勢は限定的
 非金融法人企業は資金余剰が継続
― 設備投資が高水準で推移する一方、企業部門全体では潤沢な内部資金が投資を下支え
 AI投資ブームのもと企業の外部資金調達は拡大
― 押し上げの主因は株式発行。債務調達は一定の規模を維持しているものの増勢は鈍化。投資を主導するハイパース
ケーラーの高いキャッシュ創出力に加え、株式による資金調達を反映した格好
5

### Page 7

米国：債務調達は全般に減速。企業金融で存在感高めるプライベートクレジットも拡大一服
 債務調達の内訳をみると、社債、銀行借入、プライベートクレジットとも前期から減速
― とりわけプライベートクレジットは2024～25年の拡大局面から2026年入り後は鈍化が鮮明。IT分野では成約件数が大
幅に減少、全体に占めるシェアも低下。ソフトウェア企業を巡る信用リスクへの警戒や案件組成の減速が影響した模様
 減速したとはいえ、プライベートクレジットは企業金融で一定の存在感
― 企業債務残高に占めるプライベートクレジットの割合は7％程度と銀行借入にほぼ匹敵。過去10年間では3倍に拡大
6

### Page 8

米国：AI投資の外部化でプライベートクレジットを含む案件型金融が拡大する可能性
 事業開発会社（BDC）による資金供給の縮小が際立つ。足元ではプライベート・デットファンドを通じた供給にもかげり
― プライベートクレジットの活用が限定的だった背景には、信用力の高いハイパースケーラーによるデータセンターやAI
サーバー投資が中心だったことも一因
 AI関連投資ブームのすそ野拡大に伴いプライベートクレジットなどの活用余地が高まる可能性
―SPV・JV、データセンター事業者、電力・送配電・冷却など周辺インフラへ投資主体が広がれば、個別案件のキャッシュ
フローや資産価値に応じた資金調達需要が高まることも。案件型資金調達の活用余地拡大には目配り
7
プライベートクレジットの供給主体別増減
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 9

日本：中東情勢悪化を受けて輸送コストが急騰。原油高と合わせて輸入負担を押し上げ
 原油輸入額の増加で貿易赤字が拡大
―6月以降、割高な米国産を中心に原油の代替調達が進む中で輸入金額が増加
 中東情勢悪化で原油タンカー運賃が夏場以降急騰
― 運賃は7月末から約3倍に上昇。今後、割高な米国産から中東産へと回帰する中でも輸入負担は下がりにくい可能性
― 貿易赤字が長期化し、円安が進めば、輸入物価を一段と押し上げるリスク
8

（年／月）
日本：CPIはエネルギー補助効果で小幅鈍化も、補助終了後は再加速のリスク
9
 8月の全国コアCPIは前年比＋1.7％（前月：同＋1.8％）と小幅に鈍化
― 電気・ガス料金への補助再開でエネルギー関連が下落。補助効果でCPIを0.2％Pt程度押し下げ
― 一方、既往の原油高に伴う最終財への価格転嫁が徐々に浸透するなか、ラップや洗剤等を中心にその他財価格が上昇
 こうした価格転嫁は今後も続くとみられるほか、電気・ガス料金の補助効果が剥落するもとで、エネルギー物価は再加速へ
― 原油高等が時間差を伴って電気・ガス料金に波及。補助効果が剥落する11月には、コアCPIを0.44％Pt程度押し上げへ

― また、原油価格の再上昇や輸送コスト上昇による輸入物価の上振れは、CPIの更なる押し上げ要因に
補助再開でエネルギー関連が下落

その他財価格は上昇

日本：日銀追加利上げは最短インターバル。今後も日銀は3カ月に1回程度のペースで利上げへ
10
 日銀は9月会合で利上げを決定（反対2名）。利上げは6月以来3カ月ぶりで、今次局面では最も短いインターバル
―9月会合後の会見で植田総裁は、物価押し上げから物価上振れ抑制へと「政策の局面が変化した」と説明
― 政策金利は1.25％と、日銀による中立金利推計値のレンジ下限を上回るが、金融環境は利上げ後も緩和的と評価
 今後も日銀は3カ月に1回程度のペースで利上げを続ける可能性大
― 大幅利上げや連続利上げについては物価上振れリスクが大きい場合の選択肢と説明。急激な政策変更を回避する観点

から、先手を打って利上げを実施していく姿勢を強調
物価上振れリスクへの対応から
利上げ間隔が短期化
政策金利とブレークイーブン・インフレ
（注） ブレークイーブン・インフレは基調的物価上昇率の一面を捉えていると考えられるが、
必ずしも基調的物価上昇率そのものを示す指標ではない点に留意
（出所）日本銀行、LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成 （出所） 日本銀行より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
日銀9月会合のポイント（声明文・総裁会見）
声明文
• 企業の賃金・価格設定行動の積極化や中長期的な予想物価上昇
率の上昇などを踏まえると、2％物価目標を上振れるリスクがある
• 企業間取引における価格上昇圧力が消費者段階にも波及し始め
ているほか、賃金上昇の販売価格への転嫁の動きも続いている
• 政策金利の変更後も、緩和的な金融環境は維持される
会見での注目発言
• 基調的物価上昇率が2％に接近し、政策局面が変化。物価上振れ
を回避する観点が重要に
• 原油価格の上昇やそこから波及した物価上昇圧力、AI関連需要

や円安進行が幅広い品目の物価上昇圧力につながる可能性
• 基調的物価の2％定着を確認するのにはしばらく時間がかかる
• 金融緩和の度合いは利上げにより少しずつ縮まっているが、銀行
貸し出しや資産市場は依然として緩和的
• 急激な利上げで金融環境が大きく変化することや、それによる経
済へのマイナス影響を回避する観点は重要
• 大幅利上げに追い込まれる可能性を減らすように政策を運営する
注目点②原油輸送費が急騰。貿易赤字長期化で円安圧力も

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 13 日
上週重點訊息
◼ TOPIX 啟動大規模改革：日本交易所集團 10 月 7 日公布 TOPIX 調整結果，符合新制納入標
準的成分股將由 9 月底的 1,636 檔降至 986 檔，另有 35 檔股票新納入。現有成分股中，共
有 683 檔未符合流動性及浮動市值等標準，這些股票將自 2026 年 10 月底起逐步降低權
重，並於 2027 年 10 月重新評估，屆時仍未達標者將於 2028 年 7 月底降至零。此次改革有
助於改善 TOPIX 成分股的流動性及投資價值，並促使企業更加重視市場評價。

◼ 巴西總統選舉波索納洛首輪領先魯拉：巴西最高選舉法院 10 月 5 日公布首輪投票結果，參
議員弗拉維奧・波索納洛取得 47.03%的有效票，現任總統魯拉獲得 45.16%，兩人均未取得
過半票數，將於 10 月 25 日進行第二輪決選。若最終由波索納洛當選將相較有利於巴西股市
表現。
◼ IEA 支持加速釋出既有儲油：國際能源署（IEA）10 月 7 日表示，會員國支持加快釋出 3 月
已承諾提供的儲油，並將優先增加柴油供應。截至目前，會員國已釋出約 3.25 億桶儲油；若
剩餘承諾全數執行，可再向市場提供約 1 億桶供應，有助緩解運輸業與農業面臨的燃料成本
壓力。隨各國持續釋出戰略儲備石油，將有利於在短期內壓抑全球油價進一步上行。
市場展望
◼ 標普 500 獲利預估連續上修，資訊科技為主要推升來源：標普 500 企業獲利預估連續兩季上
修，其中資訊科技產業為主要改善來源，反映 AI、雲端運算及資料中心需求仍然強勁。雖然
美國資料中心仍面臨電力與基礎設施瓶頸，但建置規模持續擴大，AI 投資也逐步延伸至記憶

體、網路設備、資安及企業應用等領域。因此，預期科技類股的獲利動能仍具支撐，具 AI 供
應鏈優勢的台股與日股亦可望延續相關成長動能。
◼ 中天期債券可望兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府與企業發債供給維持高
檔，可能使市場利率維持在較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企業獲利、
現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，較高的美國公債基準利率也使其利息收益維持一定水準。短天
期債券面臨利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較易受到公債供給及利率波動影響，因
此中天期美國公債及投資級債可望在息收與利率風險之間維持較為平衡的表現。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月通膨數據高於市場預期，CPI 月增 0.6%、年增 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增
0.3%、年增 2.6%。同月 PPI 月增 1.3%，創 2022 年 3 月以來最大增幅，年增
6.3%， 顯示通膨壓力已逐步由能源價格擴散至更廣泛領域 。 數據公布後 ， 市場一
度提高對聯準會 10 月升息的預期，但隨後多位官員釋出審慎訊號，使升息押注
降溫 。 聯準會理事鮑曼表示 ， 決策前仍有時間觀察先前升息效果 ； 舊金山聯準銀
行總裁戴莉則認為 10 月無立即升息之必要。而褐皮書顯示，美國就業市場維持
「低招聘、低裁員」格局，而高能源價格帶來的通膨壓力仍值得關注。
⚫ 中國 9 月通膨持續回升，CPI 年增 0.9%，創近三個月高點；PPI 年增 5.0%，創
逾四年最大漲幅，顯示通縮壓力進一步減輕。主要受能源與原物料價格上漲帶
動，且 9 月進出口表現優於預期，AI 產業鏈需求與貿易環境改善提供支撐；不

過，信貸增長仍仰賴政府發債與季底效應，內需與民間融資動能仍偏弱。
⚫ 日本銀行審議委員小枝淳子表示 ， 不宜對升息過於保守 ， 並認為政策利率仍有進
一步上調空間。總裁植田和男則強調維持2%通膨目標的重要性。整體而言，日
本央行持續釋出偏鷹派訊號，有助支撐日圓表現。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委溫施表示 ， 在能源價格波動下 ，為了控制通膨仍不排除進一步升息
可能，總裁拉加德則認為法國債務問題應透過財政政策解決， 歐洲央行官員普遍
認為能源價格的潛在波動依然是推升通膨並使其居高不下的主要風險來源。。
⚫ 澳洲央行會議紀錄指出，能源價格上漲與需求韌性可能使通膨更具黏性，保留
進一步升息空間。然而 9 月就業數據意外疲弱，市場大幅下調升息預期，目前
預估 11 月維持利率不變的機率接近 90%。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
美元兌新台幣匯率走勢
Oct/19/2026
＜上週走勢＞
上週台幣匯價高點為 31.890，低點在 32.520，以 32.338 作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 4 角 6.3 分。
上週台幣走貶 ， 周初 ， 外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢 ， 雖逢高吸引出口商拋匯 ，
仍不敵外資匯出力道令台幣走貶 ； 週二台股重挫近千點 ， 外資大幅賣超台股1653 億
並匯出，台幣接連跌破 32.000、32.100 及 32.200，總成交量 71.3 億，創歷史新高；
周三台股收漲，外資雙向進出，台幣收斂部分跌幅；週四，受美國通膨高於預期影
響，早盤台幣跳空開低，台股跌逾2%，外資大幅賣超 3025 億，台幣跌破 31.400 關
卡 ， 總成交量91.2 億 ， 再創歷史新高 ； 週五外資持續匯出 ， 台幣貶至周內低點32.520，
終場在央行調節之下收在 32.338。
＜未來分析＞
預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國 CPI、PPI 雙雙高於預期，通

膨壓力仍在，儘管聯準會官員陸續發言表示不急於升息，淡化市場對於聯準會十月
升息預期，惟年底前聯準會仍有升息一碼空間，利率持穩於高位將給予國際美元支
撐；就台灣市場來看，市場對AI 產業資本支出、融資成本及信用風險產生疑慮，若
賣壓延續，將壓抑市場風險偏好，增加外資調整台股部位及匯出的壓力，令台幣面
臨持續貶值的壓力，惟出口商拋匯將抑制台幣貶值速度，綜合以上，預估本週台幣
走勢偏弱。
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.100-32.700

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/19/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走升。上週人民幣中間價連續走升，週三中間價升至6.7249 創
三年八個月新高，惟市場靜待美國公布通膨數據，交投謹慎，人民幣僅小幅走升；
週三晚間，美國公布 9 月 CPI 年增率 3.7%、核心 CPI 年增 2.6%，雙雙高於預期，
帶動美元指數升至年內高點 102.945、 人民幣貶至周內低點6.7005； 然多位聯準會官
員發言表示聯準會不急於升息，令市場對於聯準會十月升息預期略有降溫，美元指
數自高點回落，激勵人民幣由貶轉升；臨近周末，聯準會主席華許於IMF 會議發言
並未指引十月聯準會政策利率走向，再度淡化市場對於聯準會十月升息預期，國際
美元走勢偏弱，人民幣走升觸及年內高點 6.6825，終場境外人民幣收在 6.6850，週
線升值 91 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6870，週線升值 60 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣震盪偏升。就國際市場來看，美國仍面臨高通膨、年底可能將
再次升息，利差因素支撐美元指數於高檔震盪，限縮人民幣升值空間；就中國國內
市場來看，中間價持續偏升將帶動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人
民幣表現；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，匯價維持在五日均線之下交
投 ， 均線呈現空頭排列 ， 加以USD/CNH 有效跌破 6.6900 整數關卡 ， 顯示USD/CNH
空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣震盪偏升。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6500-6.7000

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/19/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 先上後下。週初，受油價攀升推升美國通膨與利率預期帶動，美
元指數溫和走升創 6 月底以來新高，推升 USD/JPY 突破 158.50；週間，美國公布 9
月 CPI 高於預期，帶動美元指數升至 2025 年 5 月以來高點，也使 USD/JPY 最高來
到 158.94，然逢高獲利了結賣壓出籠使匯價回落；臨近週末，USD/JPY 一度下探至
157.53，隨後逢低買盤進場收復部分跌幅，終場收在 157.99，週線下跌 13 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、受美伊戰事推升汽柴油飆漲
與服務成本擴散影響，美國 9 月 CPI 及 PPI 月增漲幅均超出市場預期，加上初請失
業金人數連五週低於 20 萬人顯示勞動市場穩定，市場預期聯準會將於 12 月再次升
息，美日實質利差維持高檔，持續為USD/JPY 提供下檔支撐。二、被視為鴿派的日
本經濟財政大臣城內實明確表態，貨幣政策應交由日本央行自行判斷，並宣告日本
已擺脫長期通縮、轉型為增長導向經濟體。此番言論降低政治干預日銀進一步升息
的疑慮，在貨幣政策正常化的腳步下，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 的上行空間。三、就技
術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價目前交投於月線及年線之間，各天期均線高度
糾結，多空雙方於 158 關卡附近拉鋸，在有效突破季線及年線反壓或跌破月線支撐
前 ， 仍以區間震盪看待。 綜合以上 ， 預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高 。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：157.00-159.00

歐元兌美元匯率走勢  
         Oct/19/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD 震盪走升。週初，持續受法國財政赤字與歐債利差擔憂拖累，
EUR/USD 震盪走弱；週間，因美國9 月 CPI 高於預期，市場重新評估聯準會年底前
升息腳步，導致 EUR/USD 跳水最低來到 1.1112；臨近週末，儘管美國 PPI 與零售
銷售數據偏熱，市場優先反映美債殖利率回落與風險資產重新定價，美元多頭獲利
了結帶動 EUR/USD 谷底反彈 ， 最高來到1.1269， 終場收在1.1239， 週線上漲42 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間偏空震盪，主要原因如下：一、法國央行月度展望報
告指出，法國第三季 GDP 預估僅微幅成長 0.1%，印證近期高能源的成本已實質拖
累歐元區核心成員國增長。同時，歐洲央行總裁拉加德明確拒絕出手干預法債，要
求法國透過自身財政政策化解赤字困境；在明年法國大選前的財政隱憂與基本面疲
弱下，預計將令歐元上檔持續承壓。二、儘管歐洲央行管委溫施與總裁拉加德保留
12 月升息預期，加上德國基建與軍費支出為歐元提供部分下檔保護，但市場穩固預
期聯準會年底前將再升息一次。在美國經濟韌性與高利差優勢主導下，預計將限縮
歐元反彈空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 自低點反彈並站回五日線
之上，然而中長期均線下彎仍維持空頭排列，RSI 指標亦處於 50 中軸下方，顯示整
體空方趨勢尚未扭轉，反彈至月線附近恐面臨較大賣壓。綜合以上，預估EUR/USD
本週呈現區間偏空震盪的可能性較高。

                           新台幣利率走勢分析

銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     2.020~2.025% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ，本週央行連續減發定存單使市場資金寬鬆 ， 雖外資大舉匯
出，資金供給仍充裕，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週五央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元 ， 加權平均得標利率為1.58%， 低於市場
預期區間 1.60%-1.62%，較上期標售結果下跌 5.8 個基本點。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 5 年期指標券 A15108 週二搓合成交在 1.88%，後隨美債利率滑落，加上本週
2 年期公債標債結果提振市場持券信心，帶動 10 年期指標券 A15109 週四向
下成交在 2.02%~2.025%。 
在銀行去化短期資金及補充 LCR 之需求下 ， 週三2 年期公債得標利率為1.65%，
落在市場預估區間下緣 ， 投標倍數回升至2.86 倍 ， 銀行業得標比重上升至88%。
股市承壓帶動避險需求，美債利率大幅回檔，促使台債交投偏空氛圍轉趨和
緩 ， 惟在央行維持緊縮貨幣政策下 ， 買盤積極追價的意願不強 ， 預期短線台債
殖利率呈區間格局的可能性較高，關注下週 10 年券標售結果，預估 5 年券區
間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.98%~2.03%。

市場焦點
美國
• 9月綜合PMI升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速 ，新訂單及就業亦
明顯成長 ，顯示消費 、AI投資與國防支出支撐景氣動能 。不過，需求快速擴張也加劇供應鏈
瓶頸，企業投入價格升至 2022年10月以來高點，燃料、運輸及原物料成本上升 ，顯示景氣韌
性與通膨壓力同步升溫，增加Fed政策維持緊縮的壓力。
美國
期中選舉
• 目前民調顯示 ，民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ，這將有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期 ，進
而帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑 ，對長天期美債形成支撐 。此外既有資料顯示 ，選後一年美股
平均報酬率達12.4%，主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到經濟成長、企業獲利及貨
幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。

越南
• 越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數 。估計此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流
入越南。資產管理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約 25億美元。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風 ，除了能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經濟成長 ，今年以來越南成為
美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成為中國商品流入的中轉國 ，美對越的301條款
調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。

美國景氣強勢擴張，通膨與利率壓力再升
• 企業活動創逾五年新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速擴張 ：S&P
Global美國9月綜合PMI初值升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高，
且連續四個月加速成長；服務業 PMI升至58.7（59個月高點）、
製造業PMI升至57.0（52個月高點），產出亦同步加速 ，加上
訂單與就業成長皆達近年最快 ，顯示內需、AI投資及國防支出
共同支撐企業活動。S&P Global依PMI調查推估經濟年成長約
5%，Q3則上看4%，反映短期景氣動能明顯增強。
• 供給受限推高成本 ，通膨壓力再升溫： 需求快速擴張同時推高
供應鏈與產能壓力 ，企業未完成訂單升至 2022年5月以來高點 ，
供應商交貨延遲亦明顯惡化 ，反映產能不足 ，投入價格指數升

至2022年10月以來新高 ，企業主要反映燃料 、運輸、原物料
及薪資成本上升 ，顯示供給受限與能源成本可能持續向終端價
格傳導，增加通膨黏著風險並提高Fed維持緊縮政策的壓力。
• 後市觀察：數據公布後，10年期美債殖利率一度升至約 5.12%，
創2007年以來新高 ，市場對10月再升息的押注亦升溫 ，短線
需關注供應鏈壓力是否緩解 ，以及後續ISM、就業與物價數據
能否確認通膨再升溫趨勢 。投資策略建議關注利率敏感度較低
的高品質成長股或防禦型資產 ，可適度布局受惠強勁內需與 AI
／國防支出的美股大型科技與工業類股 ，並留意升息環境下金
融股的利差受益機會 ，整體建議維持分散配置並嚴控利率風險 。

Intelligence， 2026.9.24

能源成本升高推升通膨，利率維持高檔壓力升溫
• 能源與運輸成本上升，增加通膨黏著風險：中東衝突與
俄羅斯煉油設施遭攻擊 ，使全球柴油供應持續偏緊 ，燃
料價格及運輸成本同步上升；由於柴油廣泛應用於運輸 、
農業與製造業，價格上漲將透過物流及生產成本向下游
傳導。目前全球柴油庫存偏低 、煉油產能受限，若供應
中斷延續，可能使能源價格維持高檔並延長通膨壓力。
• 公債與企業債供給增加 ，長端利率壓力升高：美國財政
赤字及政府融資需求偏高 ，加上大型科技公司持續為 AI
資料中心等基礎建設籌資 ，債券供給增加使市場承接壓
力升高；在通膨預期未完全穩定的情況下 ，投資人對長
天期債券要求較高的期限溢酬 ，進一步推升長端殖利率 ，
同時AI相關企業債近期亦出現利差擴大 ，反映市場對大

量發債及資本支出回收期的關注升高。
• 資產配置面臨成長與通膨雙重拉扯： 能源價格、企業融
資成本及長端殖利率若持續處於高檔 ，將提高企業資金
成本並壓縮高估值資產的評價空間；同時 ，利率與能源
價格波動也可能擴大股 、債市震盪。因此資產配置建議
降低長天期債券對利率變化的敏感度 ，提高對通膨與利
率波動的防禦能力 ，並以現金流穩定、具備定價能力之
優質標的為核心。
資料來源：Federal Reserve Bank of St. Louis via FRED ， 2026.9.8

美國期中選舉-目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數

於11月美國期中選舉，取得國會多數席次的機率已領先共和黨，根據市場
情境分析，期中選舉不影響既有法案執行 ，但將左右未來兩年的財政預算 、
稅制改革 、政府支出及國會監督權限 。情境(1)若共和黨續掌國會 ，減稅
政策延長、產業回流、關稅及移民限制等議程有望加速推進 ，財政擴張力
道可能進一步增強 。情境(2)分裂國會 ，兩黨制衡將使重大法案推動難度
提高，新增減稅與大型財政刺激方案較難落地 。情境(3)民主黨全面掌控
國會，則可能提高對政府政策的監督力度 ，政策方向偏向提高稅負與監管 ，
壓抑企業獲利 ，使政策環境趨向平衡。

美國期中選舉-對於股債市的影響
• 對股市的影響：美國銀行分析1900至2025年共31次美國期中選舉，資料發現標普500在期中選舉前12個月平均報酬約為2.9%，
低於所有年度平均 8.9%。因為市場在選舉前往往受到政策不確定性 、政黨競爭及投資人觀望情緒影響 ，波動相對較大 。然而
此現象不代表選舉必然導致市場疲弱，因為當時的經濟環境、通膨、利率及企業獲利等因素同樣扮演重要角色。期中選舉後12
個月的股市表現通常較佳 。歷史數據顯示，選後一年美股平均報酬率達12.4%。主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到
經濟成長、企業獲利及貨幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。
• 對債市的影響：若民主黨取得國會主導權，市場普遍預期財政支出擴張幅度將較受節制 ，有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期，進而

帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑，對長天期美債形成支撐，若共和黨持續全面掌控國會，市場可能預期減稅及財政擴張政策延續，
進一步推升財政赤字預估，帶動公債殖利率走高，對長天期美債形成壓力，不過市場已部分反映相關預期，實際衝擊幅度有限。
美國期中選舉前後對於美股的影響

AI建設熱潮吞噬自由現金流
• 資本支出創新高：雲端服務巨頭(包括亞馬遜、Alphabet、微軟、Meta、甲骨文以及SpaceX等)正在全力擴充AI算力與資料
中心，2026年大型雲端服務巨頭的資本支出合計預估將接近 9,600億美元，而2025至2031年的累計AI資本支出更可能逼近
9兆美元。
• 自由現金流出現赤字： 高額的投資加上股東回報(股息與庫藏股買回)，使許多巨頭的自由現金流(FCF)轉為負值(如Alphabet、
亞馬遜、微軟、Meta及甲骨文等)，預計到2027年，部分企業將更加依賴外部融資，而非單靠內部現金生成。
• 債券發行量大增： 為了填補資金缺口 ，科技業債券發行規模急遽上升 ，2026年迄今，各幣別的投資級科技債券發行量已突
破4,000億美元，創下歷史新高。

獲利韌性與金庫深度：強勁EBITDA成為信用安全網
• 上兆美元的流動性護城河： 儘管債務增加，但科技十二巨頭合計持有近 1兆美元的等值現金，整體科技板塊流動性充裕 ，短
期內無流動性危機。
• EBITDA強勁成長消化債務：市場預期覆蓋的12家最大科技企業在2026與2027年的EBITDA合計將成長約35%，強勁的獲利
能力顯著降低槓桿比率的上升壓力，確保信評機構維護穩定的信用評級。
• 資本配置策略分化：(1)兼顧股東回報：蘋果、微軟、Meta與Alphabet在投入AI同時，仍維持大規模的庫藏股買回與股息發
放。(2)全數投入資本支出：亞馬遜將絕大部分現金導向資本支出(2026年資本支出預計達2,200億美元居首)，且不發放股息。
• 小結與投資建議：科技巨頭為維持AI技術與基礎設施領先優勢，短期內，負自由現金流將成為常態。然而，這並非財務危機

訊號，而是企業利用超強獲利能力 (高EBITDA)與龐大的現金儲備進行戰略性擴張 ，科技板塊的信用風險目前處於可控且健
康的狀態，投資建議聚焦AI變現能力與EBITDA成長，優先佈局具有強大獲利護城河且能將算力轉化為實質營收的龍頭企業 。

越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數
• 預估引導60億美元資金流入越南： 富時羅素估計，此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流入越南 。受此提振，資產管
理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約25億美元。整個指數過渡將分為四個階段推行至2027年：
今年9月率先納入10%，明年3月追加20%，最後於明年6月與9月分別完成剩餘各35%的分配。市場分析指出，隨著明年3月
配額加大，市場關注度與對本地投資人的影響將更為顯著。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風： 儘管官方早前誓言今年 GDP成長目標達10%，但中東衝突以來能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經
濟成長。此外，越南與美國的貿易協商仍在進行中，由於今年以來越南成為美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成

為中國商品流入的中轉國，美對越的301條款調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。
• 觀點：儘管短線面臨不確定性 ，但越南股市同時具備多項利多題材 ，包含企業獲利成長、公共投資加速、FDI回流及資本市
場改革等。中長期結構仍偏正向，可以衛星配置的角度布局，或是以定期定額的方式參與長期成長潛力。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本

付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

息日前一日之淨值×100%。

績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。

New capacity announcements do not overturn our bullish view.
AI demand and increasing substrate complexity should keep the
market undersupplied from 2027, supporting further pricing and
margin expansion. We raise earnings estimates and price targets
for Unimicron and NYPCB and reiterate OW on both.
More capacity, but limited near-term relief: Suppliers are stepping up investment,

but several newly announced projects are not scheduled to ramp until late 2028-30
or beyond. Our updated supply-demand model continues to indicate undersupply
from 2027 through 2030, with tighter supply in CY27-28, which should only be
more favorable to pricing. Therefore, capacity additions announced in the past few
months do not, in our view, diminish the magnitude of pricing gains over the next
two years.
CY27 pricing should accelerate, amid tighter supply-demand in CY27-28, but

visibility on the magnitude remains limited: We see ABF substrate prices rising
25%+ in CY27, below buy-side expectations of ~50%. Our supply/demand outlook
points to undersupply next year, with larger substrates, higher layer counts, and
greater manufacturing complexity further constraining usable output. Raw material
cost pass-through was a key driver of CY26 price increases, we expect supply
tightness to play a greater role in CY27. This should allow pricing to outpace cost

inflation, driving faster margin expansion. We remain constructive on pricing, but the
magnitude and timing of increases depends on customer-level negotiations.
With pricing a segment-wide tailwind, we prefer Unimicron for its stronger CY27
capacity growth: As supply tightens, the ability to deliver incremental volume
should become a key differentiator. We favor suppliers bringing capacity online in
CY27 over those whose expansion plans contribute in later years. Unimicron’s

Guangfu II ramp should add 10-15% to capacity in CY27, compared with 5-10%
growth at NYPCB through debottlenecking, giving Unimicron greater scope to
benefit from both pricing and volume growth. Unimicron is also our preferred play
heading into earnings, as more demanding expectations for NYPCB leave less room
for an upside surprise. But we remain OW on NYPCB given its networking and AMD
CPU exposure, alongside its proactive pricing strategy.
We reiterate Overweight on Unimicron and NYPCB: Reflecting stronger earnings

trajectories, we raise our EPS estimates for both companies. Based on upside to our
revised price targets, our order of preference is Unimicron (NT$1,775 PT; 24x CY28e
P/E; 41% upside), followed by NYPCB (NT$2,000 PT; 22x CY28e P/E; 39% upside).
Potential risks: 1) Weaker demand for PCs, general servers, and AI servers; 2)
meaningful capacity expansion plans; 3) T-glass shortage; 4) CoWoP displacing ABF
substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Supply/Demand Update: Undersupply Deepens in CY27–28
Our updated model points to a wider supply shortfall in CY27–28, despite additional
capacity: Incorporating the latest expansion plans and semiconductor demand forecasts,
we now expect ABF substrate demand to exceed available supply by 9.3% in CY27 and
13.7% in CY28, versus 6.2% and 9.4% previously. This represents a widening of 3.1ppt and
4.3ppt, respectively, relative to our prior estimates. We maintain our view that CY26 is a

broadly balanced market, followed by increasingly tight supply over the next two years.
The main demand upgrade comes from other ASICs, including Chinese AI chips: We
raise our shipment assumptions for this category to 6.4mn units in CY27 and 8.7mn in
CY28, from 4.6mn and 6.2mn previously. Our Nvidia GPU and server CPU forecasts remain
largely unchanged, while we lower AMD GPU estimates while also keeping CY27 Trainium
and TPU shipments largely unchanged. The tighter substrate outlook therefore reflects

stronger demand across a broader set of ASIC suppliers, rather than higher shipment
assumptions for every major AI platform. We also raise our substrate content assumptions
for select chips based on our latest supply chain checks, lifting our substrate demand
estimates across some segments, including server CPUs and networking.
Higher supply assumptions provide limited relief in CY27-28: We now include additional
capacity announcements that have been announced recently, including two additional

Kinsus factories, scheduled to come online in CY28 and CY29, beyond the CY27 expansion
already in our model. Several larger projects, however, contribute later. NYPCB’s new site
starts ramping in 2H29, while SEMCO’s Sejong expansion begins mass production in
September 2028 and its project in Vietnam is scheduled to begin operations by June
2030. These additions increase medium-term supply but do not prevent the modeled
shortfall from worsening through CY28.

Increasing substrate complexity adds to the pressure on usable output: Larger body
sizes, higher layer counts, and more demanding manufacturing processes constrain the
output available to meet demand, even as suppliers expand installed capacity. With the
supply gap widening in CY27-28, we expect tightness to become a more important pricing
driver than raw material cost pass-through. This supports our forecast for 25%+ price
increases in CY27 and further increases in CY28, alongside continued margin expansion.

For CY26, Unimicron has announced capex of NT$53.7B.

•  Guangfu II: ramp in 2027
• Yangmei II: ramp in 2028

• Yangmei IV: ramp in 2029+

NYPCB (8046.TW, OW, covered by Howard Kao)
On August 6, NYPCB announced that its Board of Directors had approved a capex budget
of NT$46.8bn (~US$1,474mn) to invest in building a new ABF substrate production site.
Our checks indicate that this production site is located in the southern part of Taiwan, and
will start to ramp up production in 2H29. Given the size of this investment, we estimate
that it will approximately double its capacity exiting CY30, relative to its CY28 capacity.

Kinsus (3189.TW, Not Covered)
In our previous model, we had assumed only one factory (k6b) coming online in CY27,
increasing capacity ~30%. However, recent checks suggest that Kinsus intends to bring
two more factories online, one in CY28 (k6c) and one in CY29 (k6d). This implies that
capacity would increase another ~25% in CY28 and ~20% in CY29, bringing Kinsus' total
ABF substrate capacity to almost double exiting CY29, relative to its CY26 capacity.

Ibiden (4062.T, covered by Shoji Sato)
Back in February, Ibiden first announced that it will invest capex of ¥500bn in the
electronics field planned through F3/29, for ABF substrate expansion. Sato-san's latest
view is that Ibiden will need a new factory building to install the equipment and facilities
that will be purchased with the ¥500bn. As a result, Sato-san expects Ibiden during its 2Q
results briefing (October 30) to announce plans to make a large capital investment,

including for a factory building with a long lead time, and raise its medium-term earnings
forecast (report here).
SEMCO (009150.KS, covered by Shawn Kim)
On September 29, SEMCO announced investment of KRW4.27 trillion (~US$3,178mn) in
Sejong and KRW2.51 trillion (~US$1,488mn) in Vietnam, for AI server semiconductor
substrate capacity expansion.
• Sejong: Investment period from September 2026 to May 2028, with mass
production starting in September 2028. Largest single-product investment to date,

serving as key hub for mass production of high-value-added, highly demanding
products, proactively responding to the increasing demand for AI server substrates.
• Vietnam: The Vietnamese subsidiary aims to complete construction and begin
operations by June 2030. It will be operated as a strategic hub to respond to global
volume demand and enhance production efficiency through a stable supply
system.
With this investment, Samsung Electro-Mechanics plans to actively respond to the

growing demand for high-value-added semiconductor substrates in high-growth markets
such as AI servers, HPC, and automotive electronics. In particular, this investment is being
pursued based on medium- to long-term cooperation with global big tech and
semiconductor customers. The investment includes customer funding support and
medium- to long-term volume guarantees; thus, the company expects to strengthen the
competitiveness of its FCBGA business based on a stable demand foundation.

LG Innotek (011070.KS, covered by Ryan Kim)

According to news reports (link), LG Innotek signed a memorandum of understanding
(MOU) with Haiphong, Vietnam, for an investment to expand its semiconductor substrate
plant. Under the agreement, LG Innotek will push to build a semiconductor substrate
plant in Haiphong.
•  Construction will begin in July 2026 and is scheduled to be completed in May
2027.
• The site spans approximately 330,000 square meters (98,000 pyeong), equivalent

to 45 soccer fields.
• According to a news report, LG Innotek will invest approximately $1 billion, or
about 1.53 trillion won, for its Phase 1 expansion plan, which mainly includes RF-SiP
(radio frequency system-in-package) and FC-CSP (flip chip-chip scale package).
• ABF substrates will be part of the Phase 2 expansion plan, but no details or
investment size has been disclosed, yet.
According to a press release from LG Innotek on September 30, LG Innotek started mass

production of ABF substrates for PC chipsets targeting big tech clients last year. It
initiated high-volume mass production of FC-BGA for PC CPUs in the third quarter of this
year. Simultaneously, the company is rapidly boosting revenue by securing additional
major clients. Starting next year, it plans to sequentially enter the AI accelerator and AI
server markets.
• Revenue from LG Innotek's package solution business has maintained an upward
trajectory for three consecutive years. With the package solution business

demonstrating tangible results alongside its optical solution business, the
company has strengthened its capacity to generate stable earnings.
• LG Innotek plans to rapidly expand its market share in the emerging semiconductor
substrate market, aiming to build its package solution business, including
semiconductor substrates, into a business generating US$743 million in operating
profit by 2031.
• In the future, LG Innotek plans to develop its future businesses, including

semiconductor substrates and physical AI solutions, to US$5.94 billion by 2030.
The company aims to have such future new businesses account for 25% of total
revenue, thereby completing a well-balanced business portfolio.
The expanded plant will produce major semiconductor substrates including radio
frequency system-in-package (RF-SiP), flip chip chip scale package (FC-CSP), and flip chip
ball grid array (FC-BGA). "The Gumi site will serve as a 'mother factory' responsible for

developing new semiconductor substrate technologies and producing new models and
high-value-added products, while the expanded plant in Vietnam will be operated as a
production base for general-purpose semiconductor substrates," an LG Innotek official
explained.

Daeduck (353200.KS, Not Covered)
Daeduck announced on July 20, 2026, that it is investing ₩497 billion (~US$370M)
manufacturing facilities and related infrastructure for semiconductor products, running

through December 31, 2027.
ABF capex is stepping up, but announced investment still falls short of implied
demand. We estimate that ABF substrate makers have announced ~US$14.3bn of
investment year to date, materially above the segment’s aggregate annual capex in recent
years, although these commitments span multiple years. Our tally is not exhaustive. LG
Innotek has yet to disclose the size of its planned ABF capacity investment, while Shinko
has not quantified the additional spending required to expand capacity following its

announced acquisition of a JDI fab.
Our supply chain checks suggest that each US$1 of investment can support US$0.50–
0.60 of annual revenue at full ramp. On this basis, the US$14.3bn announced so far could
support US$7.2–8.6bn of incremental annual revenue. This compares with US$11.4bn of
incremental revenue implied by our industry forecast. We model the ABF substrate
market reaching US$20.9bn in 2030, up from US$9.5bn in 2026. Despite the step-up in

meet our demand forecast, pointing to a need for further capacity expansion over the
coming years.

Morgan Stanley Research 13
The State of the Current Cycle
The ABF substrate market has gone through a few cycles over the past two
decades. We believe we are now still in the early innings of an up-cycle.
There was a prolonged down-cycle from 2012-18 as the PC TAM shrank amid the rise of
smartphones and tablets. During this tough period, companies were not able to generate
profits and had to compete on price to fill their idle capacity. As a result, during those

years, there was no or very limited capacity expansion, and capital expenditures by the
major ABF substrate suppliers were kept at a minimum.
Even as general server demand picked up, lifting utilization rates across the group going
into 2020, ABF substrate suppliers remained cautious on the longer-term demand outlook
and remained disciplined, hesitating to expand capacity. Hence, capex turned up just
slightly in 2019-20, increasing ~US$600mn vs. ~US$2.2bn on average per year in 2012-18.

This underinvestment led to sudden undersupply, as the shock from Covid-19 drove strong
work-from-home demand for PCs, as well as increased data usage and storage for general
computing driven by online entertainment. This ultimately led to a significant increase in
ABF substrate market value from 2020-22, reflected by the sudden surge in demand
along with meaningful pricing adjustments owing to the lack of supply.
During this 2020-22 period, much capacity expansion was set in motion as ABF substrate

suppliers and customers came to agree that necessary investments were needed for
future growth. Some subsidies were even given to these suppliers to facilitate this
capacity expansion. This is why capex jumped from ~US$2.9bn in 2020 to US$4.1bn in
2021 and US$5.5bn in 2022; even in 2023-24, capex remained elevated, as capacity
expansion plans continued.
Unfortunately, demand did not play out as expected. As the world came out of the
pandemic, demand for PCs and online entertainment fell sharply. This normalization of

demand, coupled with capacity expansion from practically every major ABF substrate
supplier, resulted in the ABF substrate market once again becoming oversupplied. This led
to a sharp decline in ABF substrate market value in 2023-24, as volumes normalized,
driving prices down to normalize against the new supply/demand dynamic.
Fortunately, cloud demand remained strong for general computing, and with AI demand
starting to pick up, the down-cycle this time around was much shorter (two years) vs. the

prior down-cycle (six years).
In 1H24, the ABF substrate market troughed and we saw an upward inflection since then,
but the pace of the recovery has been moderate and bifurcated. Midrange/low-end
markets such as PCs have shown moderate demand recovery, with high-end server and AI-
related end-demand recovering and growing at a faster pace. This is why we turned the
page on our bearish stance in 1H25, but did not turn more bullish until early 2026.

### Page 14

M
Idea
14
Periods Description
Period I (2010-2012): PC-centric
equilibrium
ABF substrate demand is primarily driven and dominated by CPU and GPU demand for desktops, notebooks,
discrete GPUs, with a meaningfully smaller exposure towards server CPUs.
Period II (2012-16): PC decline
and demand dilution
Global PC demand entered  a structural down-cycle as consumer behavior changed, with smartphones and tablets
absorbing more consumer time spent. This resulted in lower volumes and increased pricing competition as

suppliers fought to fill idle capacity.
Period III (2016-18): The quiet
inflection - servers and early
HPC
During these years, the PC market matured, while cloud computing started to take off. At the same time, server
CPU substrates quietly grew larger with more layers. We also started to see early accelerators being adopted in
high performance computing.
Period IV (2018-2022): WFH
demand driven structural
shortage
The Covid-19 pandemic drove massive work from home demand for consumer electronics and cloud computing.

This sudden surge in PC demand and increased time spent on gaming and online entertainment, bundled with a
continuous increase in substrate package size and layer count, resulted in a structural shortage, which also resulted
in longer lead times and raised prices. It was during this period (2021-22) that ABF suppliers globally started to
invest in new capacity.
Period V (2022-2024): Post-
pandemic correction and new
capacity arrival
As the world came out of the Covid-19 pandemic, WFH demand faded and  PC and cloud computing demand were

negatively affected. But at the same time, the new investments and capacity additions were just starting to slowly
come online, resulting in a drop in utilization rates and pricing.
Period VI (2024-2026):
Bifurcated recovery
PC demand normalized at the pre-Covid levels, with some replacement demand driven by Windows upgrade, while
the recovery and growth of AI and general server demand for cloud picks up at a meaningfully higher growth
trajectory.

Period VII (2026-30): AI diffusion Demand for AI proliferates from training to inference, driving ABF substrate market growth to re-accelerate over the
next five years after a two-year down-cycle following the Covid-19 pandemic. This drives increased demand for AI
computing, networking, and cloud computing, while demand for PCs remains soft.
Exhibit 18: We are entering the AI diffusion era

Source: Prismark, Morgan Stanley Research (e) estimates.
Breaking down ABF substrate end demand
The end market mix for ABF substrates has shifted dramatically over the past decade. PCs

were once the dominant demand driver, accounting for approximately 70% of global ABF
substrate market value in 2015, supported primarily by PC CPUs and GPUs. Since then,
however, the industry's demand profile has undergone a structural transformation. We
estimate that PC-related applications represented only ~23% of total ABF substrate
market value in 2025, and this share is expected to decline further to below 10% by 2030.
In contrast, demand from servers, AI GPUs, AI ASICs, and networking applications has

expanded rapidly. We estimate that these segments collectively accounted for only
around 10% of the ABF substrate market in 2015, but their share increased to ~60% by
2025. Looking ahead, we expect they will represent more than 80% of total ABF
substrate market value by 2030.
In our view, this structural shift in end-market demand has been the primary driver of ABF
substrate industry growth over the past decade and will become even more pronounced

over the next five years. As AI infrastructure deployment accelerates, we expect continued
expansion in server, AI accelerator, and networking demand to drive meaningful
acceleration in ABF substrate market growth.

Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our 12-month price target increases ~21% to NT$1,775 (from NT$1,465), driven by our
increased earnings estimates for CY26-28: We believe the company's earnings have
returned to a growth track from 2026 onward and will show stronger  growth in 2027,
driven by the high-end ABF substrate market's reversion to undersupply and Unimicron's
dominant supply share in AI ASICs and also server CPUs, which puts it in a favorable

position to benefit from rising AI investments.
We believe the overall ABF substrate market bottomed in 2H24, and see growth
accelerating from 2H26 onward with the ramp of multiple next-generation, large body size
ABF substrates for new server GPUs, CPUs, ASICs, and networking chips. New
technologies such as embedded passives and Intel's EMIB-T should also increase the
manufacturing complexity of the package substrates, which adds to the under-capacity as

usable output decreases.
Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our coverage, we use a
residual income (RI) valuation model to value Unimicron – we think it derives the most
accurate value of the firm as it takes into account cost of equity. Our key assumptions
include:
• Cost of equity of 9.2% (unchanged)
° Risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield);
° Equity risk premium of 8.7%;
° Beta of 1.0;
• Medium-term growth rate of 14% (slightly lowered from 15%)

• Terminal growth rate of 3% (unchanged, similar to the rest of the companies under
our tech hardware coverage).
Our bull and bear case values increase by similar magnitudes, to NT$3,030 and NT
$595, respectively.

Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our price target increases ~29%, to NT$2,000, reflecting our raised earnings
estimates for CY26-28: We believe the company's earnings have returned to a growth
track from 2026 onward and will show stronger growth in 2027, driven by the high-end
ABF substrate market's reversion to undersupply, NYPCB's dominant supply share for
networking chips, and its proactive pricing strategy, which puts it in a favorable position to

benefit from rising AI investments.
We believe the overall ABF substrate market bottomed in 2H24, and growth should
accelerate from 2H26 onward with the ramp of multiple next-generation, large body size
ABF substrates for new server GPUs, CPUs, ASICs and networking chips. New
technologies, such as embedded passives and Intel's EMIB-T, should also increase
manufacturing complexity of the package substrates, adding to the overall undercapacity
as usable output decreases.

Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our coverage, we use a
residual income (RI) valuation model to value NYPCB – we think it derives the most
accurate value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. Key assumptions
(all unchanged):
• Cost of equity of 9.3%;
° Risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield);
° Equity risk premium of 8.7%;
° Beta of 1.0;
• Medium-term growth rate of 17%  –  fair given our analysis of the substrate market

over the next couple of years;
• Terminal growth rate of 3% (similar to the rest of the companies under our tech
hardware coverage).
Our bull and bear case values increase by similar magnitudes, to NT$3,575 and NT
$1,020, respectively.

▪ We expect the ABF substrate market to
revert to undersupply from 2027 onward,
with the undersupply gap reaching 26% by
2030.
▪ This is primarily driven by AI demand, with
next-gen AI/networking chips adopting
larger body size ABF substrates.
▪ In the near to medium term, the short
supply of T-glass will be a key watch point,
but we believe this will mainly affect low-

end ABF and BT substrates, as suppliers
prioritize supply to the AI chip vendors.

which it traded in the previous up-cycle. We
deem this fair, given AI chip substrate spec
upgrades and suppliers' ability to raise
prices.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 124: 261016_daiwa_ Ardentec

Source PDF: all report pdf/261016_daiwa_ Ardentec.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We preview Ardentec’s 3Q26 results. In our initiation report, 
“Initiation: an emerging force in AI/HPC testing” (Sep 9), we expected 
traditional seasonality in 4Q26-1Q27 to weigh on Ardentec’s capacity 
utilisation. However, we now expect stronger-than-expected momentum in 
the networking segment to drive counter-seasonal resilience through 
1Q27E. We also expect AI revenue (with AI connectivity) contribution to 
exceed 40% in 1Q27E, well ahead of our original expectations.

What’s the impact: Robust networking demand bolsters high 
utilisation and CPO traction. Our industry checks indicate that demand 
for networking products is surging across the testing supply chain, primarily 
fuelled by the acceleration of 1.6T networking adoption. We forecast 
Ardentec’s revenue in 4Q26 and 1Q27 to beat typical seasonal slowdowns. 
As these high-speed networking programs largely follow a customer-
consignment model, their rising revenue mix should provide upside to 
overall gross margin. Concurrently, subsidiary Giga Solution is gaining 
momentum in co-packaged optics (CPO) testing, having already qualified 
and begun ramping up with leading US tier-1 players. While current 
engagement is centred on insertion 1, we see potential for customer base 
diversification and expansion into subsequent test insertions (2 and 3).

3Q26 results preview. With the Longtan fab expansion on track and 
spillover demand continuing, Ardentec 3Q26 revenue reached TWD5.34bn 
(+17.7% QoQ, +48.5% YoY), above the market consensus by 0.8% but 
slightly missing our estimate by 1.4%. With utilisation remaining high in 
3Q26, we estimate its gross margin and operating margin will reach 45.1% 
and 35.5%, respectively, above the consensus by 1.3pp and 1.9pp, 
respectively. We expect cleanroom construction of 2nd and 3rd floors will 
be finalised by year-end 2026 driven by solid domestic AI/HPC orders, with 
2nd-floor capacity likely contributing by late-1Q27.

What we recommend: We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 7% to reflect 
better-than-expected networking demand but lower our 2026E EPS by 1%. 
We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating and raise our 12M TP to TWD395 (from 
TWD340), based on an unchanged target PER of 25x on our 2027E EPS. 
While our target PER represents a premium over the OSAT/testing peer 
average, we believe this premium is justified by steady demand for high-
speed AI connectivity and the Longtan ramp-up. Our estimates indicate 
these factors could lift AI-related revenue to more than 40% of total sales 
by 1Q27. Key downside risk: delays in capacity expansion.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 7-26% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely on our more aggressive utilisation rate and margin profile.

16 October 2026

3Q26 preview: AI defies seasonality

➢ Solid AI networking demand drives counter-seasonal resilience 
➢ On-track Longtan ramp and CPO upside expand advanced testing 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; raising our 12M TP to TWD395

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Ardentec is a leading Taiwanese pure-play wafer-testing provider serving foundries, 
IDMs and fabless chipmakers. Its acquisitions of Giga Solution and Raytek over 2016-17 added RF 
testing and bumping capabilities. Combining automotive-grade reliability with flexible AI/HPC capacity, 
the company provides customers a secondary supplier which enhances supply-chain resilience.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 16 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 9 Sep 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The board demonstrates strong governance oversight, with 42% independent directors, regular
performance evaluations, and ESG-linked executive compensation. Governance quality is
further supported by a top 5% ranking in Taiwan’s corporate governance assessment for nine
consecutive years.
Capital management 2
Capital allocation appears disciplined, balancing expansion investments with shareholder
returns. The company funded major capacity growth while maintaining strong profitability and
distributed stable cash dividends, reflecting a prudent and shareholder-friendly capital
management approach.
Related party &
transaction 1
Related-party transactions appear well governed and transparent. The company states that
intercompany dealings follow arm’s-length principles and are not used for tax minimization, while
directors are required to disclose conflicts and abstain from related decisions.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Global Idea
Greater China Semiconductors
OCP Wrap-up: Rising Optics and
Al Infrastructure Alternatives
Our US trip highlighted ICA/IPO traction, complementary rack-
management silicon, hardware security and CXL-based memory
pooling, with potential storage server upside.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863
Charlie Chan
Equity Analyst

Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Henry Zhao
Equity Analyst
Henry.Zhao@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7731
Ethan Jia
Research Associate
Ethan.Jia@morganstanley.com                                                +852 3963-2287
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
ICA/IPO: Gaining traction, but cost still a hurdle. A China CSP outlined optical
form factors from XPO/NPO to interposer optics (IPO) and CPO, including

integration of IPO with interposer copper attach (ICA). TE Connectivity showcased
224G/448G NPO/CPO sockets and ICA sockets with 0.4mm pitch and up to 10,000
pins, offering high-density connectivity, low crosstalk and improved reworkability.
We see rising traction for ICA/IPO, with potential mass production in 2027   (For
more, see our recent CPO report).
Marvell IMC: Complementary to AST2700, not a replacement. Marvell
demonstrated the OCTEON-based infrastructure management controller (IMC)

addresses higher-level Al rack/cluster telemetry and control; ASPEED's AST2700
BMC continues to handle node-level security and remote management. Given
distinct functions and software ecosystems, we do not view IMC as a direct
AST2700 competitor. We also don't see an AST2700 production issue, supporting
an on-track ramp. The key debate is actually market share in TPU/Axion.
Caliptra: Expanding hardware Root of Trust opportunities. The open Caliptra

framework defines silicon-level Root of trust (RoT) for data-center processors and
accelerators, supporting secure boot, device identity and attestation eMemory
(3529.TW) subsidiary PUFsecurity offers PUFrt, providing NeoPUF-derived device,
NeoFuse OTP secure storage and hardware entropy as physical security building
blocks for Caliptra implementations. Broader adoption could support incremental
PUF/security-IP licensing, although monetization depends on qualifications and

customer design wins. (For more, see our quantum security report).
Other: CXL fabric, cooling and compute ramps: The microchannel lid
demonstration underscored the importance of chip-level thermal design as rack
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
ICA/IPO traction: China CSP and TE demonstrations suggest growing adoption,
although cost could be higher
IMC versus BMC: Marvell IMC complements AST2700; we see no production
disruption.
Hardware security: Caliptra strengthens the case for eMemory PUF-based
security IP.
CXL and thermal: CXL supports memory pooling: Cooling and ASIC/CPU ramps

remain in focus. Muse is likely driving storage server demand.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O or Over) - The stock's total return is expected to exceed the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry
team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E or Equal) - The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or

industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR) - Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's
industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U or Under) - The stock's total return is expected to be below the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry

team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

AVC (3017.TW): Liquid cooling rising penetration and AI servers ramp-up 
to drive growth ahead; Buy
19 October 2026 | 3:24AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect AVC’s monthly revenues to continue 
sequential growth through Dec 2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +42% YoY/ 
+15% QoQ, driven by: (1) new AI server racks ramp-up, driving the overall liquid 
cooling penetration rate. As we highlighted in Oct (report link), we expect AI server 
rack (NVL72-equivalent) shipment (incl. Nvidia and AMD) to reach 63k/ 110k/ 164k 
in 2026-28E, reflecting our positive view on AI server racks. (2) Its major ASIC AI 
server customer’s new model ramp-up, driving its air-cooling and liquid-cooling 
components, along with a rising liquid cooling penetration rate. (3) Its customer base 
expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further drive its liquid cooling components in 
4Q26. And (4) increasing adoption rate of ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit 
the lightweight design and major customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s 
net income to grow at a +39% CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a 
growing liquid cooling penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total 
solutions (air cooling, liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain 
Buy.

### Page 2

Earnings revisions: We factor in AVC’s Sep revenue and revise down our 2026E net 
income by -1%, mainly on lower revenues in 4Q26E, reflecting the slow consumer 
electronics under high memory costs and AI servers model transition. We revise up our 
2027-28E net income by 4% / 0%, mainly on higher revenues and GMs. Our 2027-28E 
revenue is revised up by 1% / 1%, mainly on a higher contribution from AI server liquid 
cooling components. 2027-28E GM are revised up to reflect the rising liquid cooling 
components driving profitability.

Valuation: We continue to use a near-term P/E to derive our 12-month target price and 
update our target P/E multiple to 30.6x 2027E EPS (vs. 30.3x previously), which is still 
derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. Our target P/E 
multiple is above AVC’s historical average +1stv. forward trading P/E, reflecting our 
positive view on AVC riding on the rising penetration rate of liquid-cooled AI servers. 
With our updated target P/E and earnings estimates, we raise our 12-month target price 
to NT$4,736 (from NT$4,513). Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$4,736 on a target P/E multiple of 30.6x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 136: 261020_daiwa_ZDT

Source PDF: all report pdf/261020_daiwa_ZDT.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT), the world’s largest PCB 
manufacturer by revenue, generated revenue of TWD182.5bn in 2025. 
Historically focused on consumer electronics and smartphones, the 
company is shifting towards higher-value AI-server PCBs (HLC/iHDI), 
optical-module PCBs (mSAP), and IC substrates (ABF/BT). These 
businesses accounted for 21.6% of its revenue in 1H26, up from 11.7% in 
2025. Management targets this ratio to reach c.25% in 2026 and c.30% in 
2027. To support this shift, ZDT has 22 fabs under construction and plans 
to build nine more, primarily for mSAP/HDI, HLC, and ABF substrates.

Highlights: ZDT’s “One ZDT strategy” is gradually bearing fruit. Since 
2018, management has pursued its “One ZDT strategy” to broaden the 
product portfolio – including HLC, substrates, mSAP/HDI, rigid-flex and 
SLP – by leveraging its strengths in FPCB and consumer HDI. This 
strategy is beginning to pay off, and management expects its fast-growing 
segments, including mSAP HDI for optical transceivers, AI-server HDI and 
ABF/BT substrates, to gain momentum from this year. Management 
expects revenue from these segments to increase by c.150% YoY and 
account for c.25% of total revenue (or c.TWD50bn) in 2026, and to further 
increase by 100% YoY to TWD100bn in 2027. ZDT targets these fast-
growing segments to reach 30% of its revenue mix in 2027 and c.45-50% 
in 2030. Given current progress, the 2030 target could be reached in 2028. 
For mSAP HDI, management expects the 800G/1.6T transceiver product 
mix to shift to 7:3 in 2027, from 5:5 in 2026, and aims to become the largest 
supplier of optical-transceiver HDI in 2027. In AI-server HDI, we expect the 
2 leading ASIC vendors to gradually shift from pure HLC to thick HDI 
boards – a combination of HLC and HDI – in their next-generation designs, 
with ZDT a potential beneficiary. This could drive c.3x growth in ZDT’s 
business in 2027.

Product mix to support gross margin expansion. Margin expansion is 
increasingly driven by a favourable product mix. Gross margin reached 
22.4% in 1H26 (vs. 16.5% in 1H25). Management indicated that optical-
transceiver HDIs currently have the highest gross margin, at over 40%, 
followed by IC substrates, with ABF margins potentially reaching 50-60% 
and BT margins around 30% as a long-term target. Server HLC margins 
are around 20-30%, while HDI margins should be higher given their greater 
process complexity. With margins in traditional businesses at c.20%, ZDT 
expects growth in the newer segments to drive further margin expansion.

Valuation: The stock is trading at 36/19x PER for 2026/27E based on 
Bloomberg consensus. It’s past 3-year PER range is 10-30x.

20 October 2026

Business transformation bears fruit

➢ Key beneficiary of growth in optical transceiver & AI server HDI demand  
➢ Aggressive substrate capacity strategy paying off in current conditions   
➢ Margin expansion on fast-growing segments (HDI and substrates)

Share price performance

## Document 198: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

IBKS RESEARCH │5

표 5. 삼성전자 사업부별 세부 전망

HD 현대중공업 (329180)

드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가  Flash Note 
[조선/기계/방산] 변용진 2122-9181 yongjin.byun@imfnsec.com

Check Point  Buy (Maintain) 
HD현대중공업 중형엔진 및 SMR 공장 증설 결정  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건

목표주가(12M) 860,000 원(유지) 
종가(2026.09.10) 453,500 원 
상승여력 89.6 %

HD현대중공업, 중형엔진 및 SMR 시설투자 공시  
기다리던 엔진 증설의 세부 사항이 드디어 공개되었다. HD 현대중공업은 10일 공시를 통해 총 1조 722억원(중형엔진 
8,336억원/SMR 2,386억원) 규모의 시설투자 계획을 밝혔다. 먼저 중형엔진 관련, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가에 따른 엔진발전 시장 
확대에 대응 목적으로 생산능력 확충을 위한 토지 취득 및 공장 신설 및 생산설비 구축에 28년 5월까지 투자가 진행될 예정이다. 
중형엔진의 생산 능력은 현재 연간 3GW급에서 증설이 완료되면 7.2GW급으로 확장되며, 첫 엔진 출하는 28년 하반기로 예상된다. 
증설되는 신공장의 가동률은 28년 30%로 출발하여 30년 100%로 올라설 전망이다. 이에 따라 연간 1GW당 약 1조원의 매출과 
20%이상의 영업이익(현 엔진사업부 이상) 수준으로 추정되며, 계획대로 증설이 완료되고 일감을 확보한다면 총 4조원 이상의 매출 
증대 효과가 기대된다. SMR 투자 관련해서는 SMR 주기기 제작사업 진입 및 수주물량 증가에 대응하기 위해 SMR 전용 공장, 설비 
구축 및 기존 공장 최적화에 29년 4월까지 2,386억원이 투입된다. 이는 연간으로 Terrapower의 Natrium SMR 주기기 2기(각 
345MW)를 제작할 수 있는 규모다. HD현대중공업은 이번 증설이 데이터센터용 엔진만을 위한 것이 아님을 강조했다. 데이터센터 뿐 
아니라 국가전력망, 원전용 비상발전기 등 전세계적으로 증가하는 육상용 발전엔진 수요에 대응하기 위함이며, 최근 미국의 육상 
데이터센터 사업이 님비현상 등으로 일부 좌초되고 있음에도 그보다 더 큰 그림에 베팅한 것이다.  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건  
분명한 기업가치 증분의 근거가 되는 증설공시였음에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업의 주가는 발표 이후 하락으로 마감하였다. 올해 
조선업 주가는 YTD 평균 10%이상 하락하며 약세를 보이고 있는 상태다. (HD현대중공업 -10.0%, 삼성중공업 -11.6%, 한화오션 -
25.5%)  
조선사 공히 2분기의 실적 서프라이즈와 함께 올해 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하는 등 우수한 펀더멘털을 유지하고 있으나 이는 
주가 상승재료로 반영되고 있지 않다. 7~8월 반도체발 주식시장 혼란에 따른 전체적 수급 약세도 무시할 수 없는 요인이다. 여기에 
지난해 주가 상승의 주요 동력으로 작용하였던 미국 함정 사업 관련 협력 진전이 느린 점 등이 주가 약세의 주요 원인으로 
분석된다. 백악관 및 美해군에서는 지속적으로 함정 해외 건조의 필요성을 역설하고 있으나 美입법부에서는 지역구 의원의 반대 
등으로 관련 법안의 입법 동력이 약해져 있는 상황이다. 美국회의 이번 회기(119대 회기, ~2027년 1월 3일까지)가 이미 막바지에 
다다르고 있으며 11월 중간선거 일정까지 감안하면 실질적으로 이번 회기중에 관련 법안이 통과 및 발효될 가능성은 미미하기 
때문에, 2026년 남은 하반기에 함정사업 관련 조선업 주가 동력을 기대하기는 힘들어졌다. 다가오고 있는 3분기 실적도 원화 강세 
및 여름휴가/사고 등에 따른 조업일수 하락, 후판 유통가 상승 등 감익 요인이 산적해 있어 2분기에 비해 쉽지 않은 상황이다.  
그러나 여전히 본업인 조선업의 체력은 훌륭하다. 전세계 선박 발주량은 8월 누적기준 5,972만 CGT로 전년동기 대비 +60.6% 많은 
발주가 나오고 있으며 이는 2021년 이후 가장 강한 발주세다. 한국 조선사의 주력 선종인 LNG, 탱커, 컨테이너선의 발주 비중이 
전체 발주량의 67%를 차지하고 있으며 이에 힘입어 조선사들은 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하였다. (상선기준, HD현대중공업 달성 
130.6억 달러/목표 114.7억, 삼성중공업 달성 61억/목표 57억, 한화오션 달성 60억/목표없음) 3분기 실적은 다소 쉬어갈 수 
있으나 여전히 수주잔고에 기반한 추정상 매출/영업이익은 2028년까지 꾸준한 상승이 예상된다. 작년보다 오히려 하락한 주가가 
현저한 저평가일 수밖에 없는 이유다. 조선업 비중확대 및 HD현대중공업 Top Pick 의견을 유지한다.

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속원-_fba73583.pdf

### Page 1

2026.09.21   
LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속 Company Brief

3Q26 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 영업이익 시장 기대치 상회 전망 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 8.8조원(+45% YoY, + 16% QoQ), 영업이익 3,690억원(-
39% YoY, +226% QoQ)을 기록하며 원/달러 환율 하락으로 매출은 시장 기대치를 
소폭 하회하겠으나 영업이익은 상회할 것으로 전망된다. AMPC 는 미국 ESS 생산량 
증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 힘입어 전분기 대비 약 57% 증가한 3,790억원으로 
추정된다. 미국 상호관세 위법 판결에 따른 관세 환급금이나 완성차 업체들의 최소 
구매 물량 미달 보상금 반영 여부에 따라 추가적인 이익 개선 가능성도 존재한다. ESS 
부문은 전분기에서 이연된 물량의 출하가 본격화되면서 3Q26 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
38% 증가할 것으로 예상된다. 특히 미국 ESS 생산라인의 가동 안정화와 AMPC 수혜 
확대에 힘입어 수익성도 점진적으로 개선될 것으로 보인다. 자동차전지 부문도 
3Q26부터 회복세가 강화될 것으로 예상된다. 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 
재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 큰 폭으로 증가하고, 고정비 부담 완화로 적자폭도 
뚜렷하게 축소될 전망이다. 소형전지 부문은 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 
안정적인 출하 증가로 매출 회복과 수익성 방어가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 
4Q26 대규모 ESS 신규 수주 기대 
전세계 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 힘입어 빠르게 
성장하고 있다. 시장조사기관 SNE리서치에 따르면 1H26 전세계 ESS 배터리 
출하량은 461GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 71% 증가했다. 이 중 북미 출하량은 
76GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 83% 증가했으며, 분기별로는 1Q26 35GWh(+91% 
YoY), 2Q26 41GWh(+76% YoY) 를 기록했다. 통상 하반기에 출하가 집중되는 
계절성을 감안하면 올해 북미 ESS 배터리 출하량은 전년 대비 약 70% 증가한 
170~180GWh에 이를 것으로 전망된다. 연초 동사가 예상한 올해 북미 ESS 수요 
성장률 10% 초반 수준을 크게 상회할 가능성이 높은 상황이다. 올해 동사의 ESS 
신규 수주 목표는 약 90GWh이며, 현재까지 약 30GWh의 수주를 확정한 것으로 
파악된다. 이에 시장 일각에서는 연간 수주 목표 달성 여부에 대한 우려가 제기되고 
있다. 그러나 당사가 파악한 바에 따르면 대규모 ESS 신규 수주가 4Q26에 집중될 
것으로 예상된다. 특히 북미 ESS 수요의 가파른 증가세와 투자세액공제(48E)에 
연계된 PFE 공급망 규제 시행은 동사의 수주 확보에 우호적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 향후 국내 배터리 셀 업체들의 
북미 ESS 신규 수주가 지속적으로 확대될 전망이다. 미국 ESS 시장에서는 신재생 
에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 따른 수요 증가, 중국 공급망에 대한 규제 
강화가 맞물리면서 국내 업체들에 우호적인 사업 환경이 조성되고 있다. 동사 역시 
ESS 중심의 생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화될 경우 매출 성장과 함께 고정비 
부담 완화, AMPC 수혜 확대에 따른 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 이를 바탕으로 동사의 
영업이익은 2026년 약 0.8조원에서 2028년 약 5.1조원으로 가파르게 증가할 것으로 
전망된다. ESS의 역할이 신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AIDC의 안정적인 
전력 공급을 지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 
이에 따라 동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 
산업의 성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다고 판단된다. 업종 내에서 실적 
개선 가시성과 정책 수혜가 뚜렷한 동사에 대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다.

당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

Editorial 
A Fed credibility-induced bounce  
Strategy summary:  The Fed’s unanimous decision to hike 
rates this week for the first time since 2023 , and pencil in 
another hike by December, confirms our expectations – flagged 
in the last two weeklies - of a central bank willing to establish its 
independence against political pressure. The Fed’s moderately 
hawkish pivot is helping restore its inflation-fighting credentials. 
The BoJ also hiked rates this week. It is the first time on record 
all three major central banks – Fed, ECB and BoJ – have hiked 
in the same month , as they respond primarily to a resurgence 
in energy-driven inflation amid tight job markets.  
We expect the Fed to hike once more by year-end to 4.0-4.25% 
and follow up with another hike in H1 2027 to 4.25 -4.5%. With 
the revised estimates, we revise up our US government bond 
yield forecasts. Nevertheless, with Fed policy uncertainty 
easing, we see an opportunity to use the equity market dip to 
add exposure amid solid corporate earnings.  
Fed moves to restore credibility:  The Fed’s policy pivot this 
week followed Chair Warsh’s hawkish Jackson Hole speech 
and recent economic data prints, namely: a pick -up in job 
creation and inflation over the summer and a sharp rebound in 
oil prices due to the re -escalation of the Middle E ast conflict. 
Given the unanimous Fed decision to hike rates this week, we 
expect two more hikes by  June 2027 on the back of  robust 
growth amid strong AI investments and a tight labour market, 
keeping inflation elevated. We expect this pivot to help the Fed 
rebuild credibility as an inflation fighter.  
Policy outlook less aggressive than market pricing : The 
updated Fed policy rate estimates are lower compared with 
money market pricing of three more rate hikes by June 2027 
since we expect disinflation to take hold by Q2 2027 as the 
impact of oil prices and tariffs fade. We believe policy rates can 
return to the 4.0–4.25% range by end-2027, with the possibility 
of cuts in H2 2027 as inflation pressures ease.  
Upgrading near-term US rates and USD estimates : Based 
on these revised estimates, we also upgrade our 3-month target 
for the US 10 -year government bond yield to 5.0 -5.25%. We 
also revise up our 12 -month target for the US 10 -year bond 
yield to 4.75-5.0% (see page 4).  
Buying the equity dip as focus turns to fundamentals: Fed 
hikes typically do not hurt equities over the medium-term when 
accompanied by strong earnings growth. We estimate a 25bps 
rise in Fed rates (the discount rate) would impact the S&P500 
index and global equities by 3 -4%. However, we expect the 
headwind from higher rates to be offset by strong earnings 
growth, driving positive performance for global equities.  
Furthermore, a hawkish Fed is largely priced in and we expect 
the Fed to deliver fewer rate hikes than currently priced by 
markets. Meanwhile, we believe concerns over AI industry 
leaders slowing the pace of development are overdone. Hence, 
we maintain our preference for a diversified group of large cap 
technology and semiconductor industry leaders (see page 5-6). 
BoJ hikes; modestly bullish JPY : The BoJ hiked rates by 
25bps to a three-decade high of 1.25%, as expected, but the 7-
2 split vote led to renewed JPY weakness on concerns it may 
slow the pace of further hikes. We remain constructive on JPY 
amid narrowing rate differential vs peers as the BoJ potentially 
hikes rates by 25bps once per quarter until Q2 2027 to curb 
rising domestic wage-driven inflation pressure . Any sign of BoJ 
slowing the pace of hikes could lead to renewed JPY weakness, 
fuelling inflationary pressures (see page 7).  
BoE holds; rangebound GBP: The BoE held rates this week, 
bucking the hiking trend among peers, as weakening UK job 
and housing markets offset rebound in energy-driven inflation. 
We expect BoE to deliver fewer rate hikes than the 100bps of 
increase currently priced by markets  as the job market slows 
further. This is likely to keep GBP rangebound (see page 7).  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions 
  Where do you see US government bond  yields following 
the Fed’s 16 September decision to hike interest rates? 
Our view: The US 10-year government bond yield is likely to 
retest the 5.00-5.25% range in the near term. We favour 3-7-year 
maturity bond s in t he US, preferring credit over government 
bonds. We are looking for an opportunity to extend duration 
approaching Q2 2027. 
Rationale: Over the next few months, we expect 2- and 30-year US 
government bond yields to remain rangebound, but the 10-year yield 
is expected to rebound up to the 5.00-5.25% range. The reasons for 
this include: 1) oil prices staying elevated and potentially rise further 
due to the fluid geopolitical situation in the Middle East, with Saudi 
Arabia’s East-West oil pipeline shut, the Houthis now firmly in control 
of the Strait of Bab-al-Mandab, the Strait of Hormuz still closed and 
China returning as a buyer of crude oil in the market ; 2) despite 
notable AI leaders expressing the need to slow AI development, AI 
capex plans are showing no signs of slowing down, which continues 
to drive above-trend growth in the economy. This, in turn, also means 
AI-related heavy bond issuance  is expected to continue. These 
issuers typically issue long-dated tenors, and this competition for 
capital will also exert upward pressure on the US 10-year yields. 
We note that the US 10-year bond yield has room to move lower, 
driven by short-covering activities in the very near term as net-short 
positions had moved to extreme levels before th is week’s  Fed 
meeting. However, we think such downshifts would be short-lived 
and the central tendency for the 10-year yield would be to move back 
up to the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Meanwhile, we see US 30-year yields continuing to reflect the dire 
fiscal outlook for the US government. We expect US 2-year yields 
to continue trading in a range that reflects expectations of further 
federal funds rate hikes. For the record, we only expect two more 
rate hikes from the Fed into the middle of 2027 , while money 
markets are pricing in three more for the same period. 
Going into H2 2027, we expect oil prices to moderate (either 
through a resolution of the M iddle East conflict or a re-routing of oil 
supply chains)  and the inflationary effects from tariffs to fade. 
Inflation by then is likely be close to – if not at – the Fed’s target of 
2%. At this point, we see room for the Fed to contemplate rate 
cuts. US 2-year yields (the tenor that is most sensitive to Fed policy 
changes) will likely start to price th is in. With oil prices and inflation 
cooling, 10-year yields would also have room to come down. 
US 30-year yields, on the other hand, are expected to  remain 
elevated as the US fiscal deficit remains substantial next year and 
could become much worse than its already dire state today. As a 
result, the ‘term premium ’ may keep the ultra -long end elevated. 
Short of some unorthodox policy to artificially cap them lower, 30 -
year yields could continue to drift higher into H2 2027. 
We continue to favour the 3-7-year segment of the yield curve and 
prefer credit over government bond s. We see an opportunity for 
us to extend duration closer to Q2 2027. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO for Fixed Income and FX

We have updated our three- and 12-month outlook 
for US government bond yields

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Will the step -up in the  Fed’s risk-free rate trigger a re -
pricing of equities? 
Our view: We expect strong corporate earnings growth to offset 
the headwind of higher rates , driving positive performance for 
global equities. We remain Overweight global equities. 
Rationale: The Fed’s latest 25bps rate hike had been anticipated 
by the bond market, partly reflected in the bond yield  rise we have 
seen so far in 2026. Using the US 10-year government bond yield as 
a proxy for the risk -free rate, the yield has risen by 80-85bps this 
year. We estimate that each 25bps rise in the discount rate presents 
a valuation drag on global equities. On this basis, the rise in yields 
represents a potential 10-14% headwind for equities this year. 
However, the Fed’s hike reflects a solid expansion of US economic 
activity, resilient domestic spending and robust capital investment. 
This is consistent with the positive earnings revisions we have seen 
this year, with  2026 earnings growth projections now at 34% (and 
16% for 2027). Global equities are up 14% year-to-date (YTD), but 
even with a 14% valuation headwind from higher yields, we believe 
they can rise further, given strong earnings growth in 2026 and 2027. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist 
  What is the outlook for the US 10-year government bond 
yield? What is your tactical view on the DXY? 
Our view: We raise our three-month US 10 -year government 
bond yield forecast to the 5.00-5.25% range. Meanwhile, we see 
a near-term upward bias in the DXY after its recent downtrend, 
supported by recent Fed tightening and higher  short-term US 
bond yields.  
Rationale: The Fed 's decision  to raise the target range for the 
federal funds rate by 25bps to 3.75-4.00% validates the upside policy 
risks we flagged recently, following Fed Chair Warsh's Jackson Hole 
speech and a run of firmer US economic data. We expect growth 
to remain robust, the labour market  to remain tight and inflation to 
stay elevated, supporting one more hike before year-end 2026 and 
potentially another in H1 2027  – fewer than the three additional 
hikes currently priced in by money markets . For the US 10-year 
government bond yield, further policy tightening and lingering US 
fiscal concerns should keep yields elevated, but the Fed’s visible 
response to inflation should limit additional upward pressure, leaving 
the 10-year yield holding around the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Crucially, Warsh provided no forward guidance on further rate 
hikes. Markets had already priced in substantial additional rate hikes 
ahead of the Fed meeting, reducing the scope for further USD upside 
purely from rate repricing. Meanwhile, we expect further policy 
tightening from the ECB and the BoJ, while the RBA also remains 
biased towards further tightening. This should gradually narrow the 
US rate advantage and limit the extent of a DXY rebound. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Anthony Naab, CFA, Investment Strategist

Global equities’ earnings growth estimates have 
been revised higher this year, offsetting the 
headwind from higher rates

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in technology as AI lab 
CEOs call for a slowdown in frontier AI model development? 
Our view: We believe concerns over “pacing the frontier” are 
overdone. We maintain our preference to remain invested in 
and diversified across Big Tech and semiconductor leaders. 
Rationale: We believe it is prudent for investors to avoid 
overreacting to the recent calls for a slowdown in frontier AI model 
development, as they focus on responsible AI development, rather 
than halting AI model development or reducing spending. The global 
AI leadership  race, reinforced by the US and China’s  strategic 
ambitions, also makes a coordinated slowdown unlikely. 
While the near-term narrative may shift from AI training beneficiaries 
towards inference plays, hyperscalers continue to signal strong AI 
infrastructure commitments and improving monetisation, supporting 
our AI capex outlook. Rising AI adoption and agentic use cases 
should sustain inference demand, even if training activity moderates, 
benefiting custom AI chips, NAND flash memory and hyperscalers.  
We believe the decline in valuations  this year more than 
compensates for any downside risk to AI capex. Our Opportunistic 
idea on Global Semiconductors further reflects our conviction, with 
attractive valuations supporting selective additions during volatility. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  With ASEAN and UK equities outperforming AI-focused 
markets in H2 2026, is it worth adding to these markets? 
Our view: ASEAN and UK equities offer portfolio diversification, 
but we are Underweight both markets in favour of US and Asia 
ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, given their stronger earnings potential. 
Rationale: The ASEAN and UK markets have outperformed the 
MSCI ACWI over the past three months, but both have 
underperformed on a YTD basis. Financials is the largest sector in 
the ASEAN (53%) and the UK (28%) and provides steady earnings. 
However, both markets have minimal exposure to the technology 
sector (just 3% and 1% , respectively), ultimately dragging down 
growth. Hence, while defensive markets offer value by increasing 
diversification and reducing volatility in a portfolio, we maintain our 
Underweight allocation for ASEAN and the UK, as we see much 
stronger earnings potential elsewhere in the US and AxJ markets.  
We remain Overweight US and AxJ, supported by robust earnings 
growth, as  AI still has the potential to become a major economic 
growth driver . Our 12-month S&P500 Index forecast of 8,400  
implies low-teens upside, supported by a strong earnings backdrop 
of 25-30% earnings-per-share growth for 2026 and 15 -20% for 
2027. AxJ also boasts the strongest earnings growth among major 
markets, fuelled by the AI semiconductor super-cycle. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Standard Chartered Bank
